                function langToggle() {
                    var lang = this.getAttribute('data-lang'),
                    	fallback = this.getAttribute('data-href');

                    for (var i = 0; i < lObj.length; i++) {
                    var isLangObject = lObj[i].langCode,
                        isCurrentCode = lObj[i].currentLangCode;

                           }

                              window.location.replace(lObj[i].langHref +  '?' + myParams);
                           }
	                        return false;

	                    }
                    }
                };

2. MUST BE A CURRENT OWNER OF A 2005 MODEL YEAR OR NEWER NON-GM VEHICLE FOR AT LEAST 30 DAYS PRIOR TO NEW VEHICLE SALE. Based on MSRP of $26,910. Not available with special finance, lease and some other offers. Take retail delivery by 10/31/19. See participating dealer for details.</p>

				staticImgs = document.querySelectorAll('.q-mod-static-image');
				viewportWidth = window.innerWidth,
				re = /^\(min-width: (\d+)px\)/,
				imageHeight = 0,
				isIE = !!window.navigator.userAgent.match(/(?:msie |trident.+?; rv:)(\d+)/i);

			// Loop through the sources to identify each image at what viewport to determine which aspect ratio to use
			// Then, once aspect ratio is found based on which image (primary or secondary) set the height of the
			// image container to the calculated height based on width and aspect ratio
			for (var i = 0; i < pics.length; i++) {
				var sources = pics[i].getElementsByTagName('source'),
					img = pics[i].getElementsByTagName('img'),
					parentEl = pics[i].parentElement,
					aspectratioToUse = 0;

				// Do not need to set the height if image height control is enabled
				if (parentEl.getAttribute('data-enable-image-height-control') === 'true') {
					continue;
				}

				// IE does not support source so skip right to fallback
				if (!isIE) {
					for (var j = 0; j < sources.length; j++) {
						var media = sources[j].getAttribute('media'),
							mediaElements = media ? media.match(re) : undefined,
							minWidth = mediaElements && mediaElements.length === 2 ? mediaElements[1] : 0,
							imageAspectRatio = sources[j].getAttribute('data-aspectratio');

						if (!imageAspectRatio || viewportWidth < minWidth) {
							continue;
						}

						if (aspectratioToUse <= 0) {
							aspectratioToUse = imageAspectRatio;
						}
					}
				}

				imageHeight = aspectratioToUse > 0 ? (pics[i].offsetWidth / aspectratioToUse) : 0;

				if (imageHeight > 0) {
					pics[i].style.height = parseInt(imageHeight + .5) + 'px';
				}
			}

			// Static Images
			for (var i = 0; i < staticImgs.length; i++) {
				var img = staticImgs[i].getElementsByTagName('img')[0],
					imageHeight,
					aspectratioToUse;

				if (!img.style.height) {
					aspectratioToUse = img.getAttribute('data-aspectratio');

					imageHeight = aspectratioToUse > 0 ? (staticImgs[i].offsetWidth / aspectratioToUse) : 0;

					if (imageHeight > 0) {
						img.style.height = parseInt(imageHeight + .5) + 'px';
					}
				}
			}
		</script>
